# MS.990 Parallel Edition: Canvases 635–664

## Page 698 — IIIF Canvas #635

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0635.JP2` (2400 × 3051 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0635.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0635.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Page 698 (recto) continues the decoction and sweating regimen from p. 697 in the left column, followed by mercurial salve recipes for smearing. The right column provides gargles, liniments, and a mercurial-sublimate water for treating throat and palate ulcers caused by Morbus Gallicus, instructions on the duration of salivation smearing, and a rubricated section listing diagnostic signs of the disease.

## Page 699 — IIIF Canvas #636

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0636.JP2` (2401 × 2672 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0636.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0636.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Page 699 (verso) continues describing the progression, contagion, and clinical distinctions of Morbus Gallicus in the left column, warning of infection via breath, sweat, and contaminated clothing. The right column presents a series of alternative cures, including mercurial pills, Turpethum minerale boluses, white and red precipitate pills, a mercury-sublimate water cure without salve smearing, several wood decoctions (apozemas), and sweat-driving remedies.

Recipes for Spanish/Roman wound balsam ('Spanischer Wundt Balsam', 'Ein Anderer Khöstlicher Wundt Balsam', 'Khöstlicher Balsam zuo den alten Schäden') with a watercolor illustration of three blue-grey apothecary bottles at the top of the left column.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Three blue-grey apothecary bottles on a shelf** (`#xywh=400,400,500,1000`): Watercolor illustration at the top of the left column depicting three blue-grey glass apothecary vessels resting on a brown shelf/table with small tufts of foliage: a square-shouldered bottle on the left, a rounded pear-shaped flask in the center, and a square-shouldered bottle on the right.

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0640.JP2` (2401 × 2689 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0640.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0640.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

## Page 704 — IIIF Canvas #641

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0641.JP2` (2400 × 3068 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0641.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0641.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Continuation of wound-drinks (including a drink for heavy falls), followed by a major rubricated section on proven plasters (Von Villen Köstlichen Chur beruenden Pflastern) accompanied by a watercolor illustration of an apothecary/surgeon stirring a steaming pot of red salve beside a table of plaster rolls.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Apothecary preparing a plaster over a brazier** (`#xywh=400,400,500,1000`): Framed rectangular watercolor illustration at the top of the right column. A bearded man in a black cap, reddish-pink patterned doublet, green breeches, red stockings, and black shoes stirs a steaming yellow-rimmed pot containing a red salve over a grey three-legged stove/brazier. To his right stands a wooden table bearing six rolled plaster cylinders (three reddish-brown and three white/grey). A doorway/window on the left shows blue sky and a yellow-brown hill.

### Left Column

| Line | German Diplomatic (Original) | English Translation & Editorial Notes |
| :---: | :--- | :--- |
| 1 | hernach dz: in MB: mit gutten △ | afterwards this: in a water-bath with good fire *— Note: Strikethrough over two words in middle; alchemical symbol △ (fire)* |
| 2 | diese ▽ füllt den grund auß wan einer | this water fills out the base when someone *— Note: Alchemical symbol: ▽* |
| 3 | wund ist ~ | is wounded ~ |

## Page 706 — IIIF Canvas #643

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0643.JP2` (2400 × 3029 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0643.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0643.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Recto page 706 (pencil foliation 318) containing German and Latin pharmaceutical plaster recipes with red-ink rubricated headings, red vertical margin and column divider rules, and horizontal section dividers. Recipes include a Diaphoretic Plaster, a Precious Spleen Plaster, a Plaster for Cancer, Fistula and All Sores, a Plaster for Opening Abscesses, a Precious Plaster for Boils/Swellings, and a treatise on the four virtues of a good stitch plaster.

## Page 707 — IIIF Canvas #644

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0644.JP2` (2400 × 2722 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0644.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0644.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Verso page 707 continuing the treatise on the stitch plaster and presenting its preparation ('Aus Pflaster mach also'), followed in the right column by a plaster that draws heat and poison from a wound, a Noble Stitch Plaster of a Noble Surgeon, and a plaster for scald-head (Erbgrund). Features red-ink rubricated headings, red column/margin dividers, and apothecary/alchemical symbols.

## Page 708 — IIIF Canvas #645

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0645.JP2` (2400 × 3032 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0645.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0645.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Recto page 708 (pencil foliation 319) featuring a watercolor illustration of an apothecary plaster-spreading board/tray with colored plaster sticks at the top left, followed by a new section of 'various precious and healing plasters': Capuchin plaster, Precious Mother Plaster, plaster for quotidian fever, softening plasters for hard swellings/spleen and scrofula, Censure plasters, toothache plaster, Doctor Hausenbach's plaster, Purgative Plaster, and Helping Plaster.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Watercolor illustration of an apothecary plaster-spreading board or tray** (`#xywh=400,400,500,1000`): Hand-drawn watercolor illustration at the top of the left column depicting a rectangular tan/ochre board or tray with a handle on the right (featuring a circular hanging hole), containing six vertical rectangular plaster sticks or bars in shades of red, orange-red, and yellow-ochre, alongside two dark oval weights or pellets.

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0646.JP2` (2400 × 2718 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0646.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0646.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Two-column page of German and Latin pharmaceutical plaster recipes (continued from previous page), including a pain-soothing plaster, a glassy/hyaline plaster for French sores and scabs, a Roman wonder plaster, a red minium/brick-dust plaster, a frogspawn plaster, a Pamphilon plaster for old weeping sores, and a Nuremberg plaster.

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0647.JP2` (2400 × 3094 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0647.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0647.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Two-column page of German and Latin pharmaceutical plaster recipes, continuing the Nuremberg plaster from page 409, followed by plasters for swelling/defensives, grey plaster for sores, earache plaster, drawing plaster for gout/sciatica, nerve-strengthening plasters, and a sciatica plaster.

## Page 411 — IIIF Canvas #648

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0648.JP2` (2400 × 2722 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0648.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0648.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Two-column page of German and Latin pharmaceutical plaster recipes, including an ash-fungus plaster for headaches and ear fluxes, an adhesive plaster, another Nuremberg plaster, a plaster for corns, a diaphoretic plaster, a minium plaster, a precious plaster for puncture wounds and sores, a plaster for ruptures, and a precious plaster for old weeping sores with instructions on preparing the stone.

## Page 712 — IIIF Canvas #649

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0649.JP2` (2401 × 3062 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0649.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0649.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Early modern German medical and surgical recipe collection (pages 712 / fol. 321r) containing plaster recipes (calamine/ore preparation, blister-healing plaster, plaster for wasting limbs, sciatica and wound plaster, bone-fracture plaster) in the left column, and a watercolor illustration of four apothecary ointment jars followed by ointment recipes (coronal suture ointment for catarrh, kidney inflammation ointment, bdellium ointment, Agrippa's ointment, Aragon ointment) in the right column.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Four apothecary ointment jars** (`#xywh=400,400,500,1000`): Watercolor illustration at the top of the right column depicting four blue-grey round-bellied apothecary jars with dark scrollwork decoration, standing in a row on a yellow-ochre ledge.

### Left Column

| Line | German Diplomatic (Original) | English Translation & Editorial Notes |
| :---: | :--- | :--- |
| 1 | Das Erzt zu den Pflastern mach also | Make the ore/calamine for the plasters thus *— Note: Rubricated heading in red ink; faded* |
| 2 | ℞. Calcinat. q. v. giess süss Frisch ▽ darauf, so | ℞. Calcined [ore/calamine], as much as you wish; pour sweet fresh water upon it, thus *— Note: Starts with apothecary symbol ℞; contains alchemical symbol ▽ (water)* |
| 3 | trübt sich das ▽ und ▽ wirt aufschliessen, giess ab, und | the water becomes turbid and the water will dissolve it; pour off, and *— Note: Contains alchemical symbol ▽ (water) twice* |

## Page 713 — IIIF Canvas #650

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0650.JP2` (2401 × 2738 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0650.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0650.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Early modern German medical and surgical recipe collection (page 713 / fol. 321v) containing ointment recipes: Alabaster ointment, Anodyne ointment, Narcotic ointment (with a second variant), and Martiaton ointment in the left column; Cordial ointment, ointment for catarrh, ointment for sciatica, Unguentum Aegyptiacum, Digestive ointment, Unguentum mundificativum (cleansing ointment), and ointment for haemorrhoid pains in the right column.

## Page 714 — IIIF Canvas #651

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0651.JP2` (2400 × 3038 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0651.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0651.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Early modern German medical and surgical recipe collection (page 714 / fol. 322r) containing ointment recipes: Unguentum mundificativum (with a second variant), ointment for chapped lips, cooling ointment (Unguentum frigidum), Unguentum de Fio, ointment for powder or other burns, and eye ointment/collyrium for fire injuries in the left column; ointment for frostbite, tobacco ointment, ointment for stubborn sores, toad ointment (Kröten Salm), and bitter orange ointment in the right column.

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0652.JP2` (2401 × 2732 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0652.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0652.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Two-column Early New High German apothecary recipe page (page 715, recto) with rubricated red-ink headings, continuing a sleep/worm salve and presenting recipes for Fire Ointment, Laxative Ointments, Scabies/Itch Ointments, Rickets Ointment, White-Lead Ointment, Rose Ointment, Drying Ointment, Tutty Ointment, Yellow Drawing Salve, Defensive Ointment, and Red Ointment.

## Page 718 — IIIF Canvas #655

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0655.JP2` (2400 × 3091 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0655.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0655.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 3,200

Recto folio (p. 718, pencil foliation 324) containing surgical and dermatological salves in two columns: continuation of a verdigris salve, a vinegar-and-honey salve better than Unguentum Aegyptiacum, Basilica salve for unhealing sores, two liniments for hemorrhoid pain, a cooling salve for inflamed sores, Unguentum digestivum, a salve for unclean sores, Unguentum von Ey (egg unguent) for scabies and ringworm, scabies salve, whitlow/hangnail salve, tobacco salve for all sores and wounds, and the beginning of a salve for sciatica.

## Page 725 — IIIF Canvas #661

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0661.JP2` (2400 × 3074 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0661.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0661.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Full-width watercolor illustration of a naked wounded man in a landscape, followed by a two-column treatise on wounds of the head, the seven anatomical layers of the head (epidermis, membrana carnosa, pericranium, eight cranial bones, dura mater, pia mater, and brain), and methods of diagnosing cranial fractures.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Wounded man in a landscape** (`#xywh=400,400,500,1000`): Full-width watercolor illustration framed in red-ink rules depicting a naked bearded man seated on a stone block in profile facing right with outstretched hands, marked with numerous red/purple cuts and wounds across his head, face, torso, arms, and legs, set against a landscape with blue hills, a gabled building, a round tower, and a tree.

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0662.JP2` (2401 × 2702 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0662.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0662.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200
